## Development

When starting the dev server, use background mode:

```
astro dev --background
```

Manage the background server with `astro dev stop`, `astro dev status`, and `astro dev logs`.

## Documentation

Full documentation: https://docs.astro.build

Consult these guides before working on related tasks:

- [Adding pages, dynamic routes, or middleware](https://docs.astro.build/en/guides/routing/)
- [Working with Astro components](https://docs.astro.build/en/basics/astro-components/)
- [Using React, Vue, Svelte, or other framework components](https://docs.astro.build/en/guides/framework-components/)
- [Adding or managing content](https://docs.astro.build/en/guides/content-collections/)
- [Adding styles or using Tailwind](https://docs.astro.build/en/guides/styling/)
- [Supporting multiple languages](https://docs.astro.build/en/guides/internationalization/)

// @ts-check
import siteConfig from "./site.config";

import { defineConfig } from "astro/config";

import tailwindcss from "@tailwindcss/vite";
import sitemap from "@astrojs/sitemap";
import mdx from "@astrojs/mdx";
import icon from "astro-icon";
import { satteri } from "@astrojs/markdown-satteri";

// https://astro.build/config
export default defineConfig({
  markdown: {
    processor: satteri({
      hastPlugins: [
        {
          name: "github-alerts",
          element: {
            filter: ["blockquote"],
            visit(node, context) {
              const firstParagraph = node.children.find(
                (child) => child.type === "element" && child.tagName === "p",
              );
              if (
                firstParagraph?.type !== "element" ||
                firstParagraph.tagName !== "p"
              ) {
                return;
              }

              const firstText = firstParagraph.children[0];
              if (firstText?.type !== "text") return;

              const match = firstText.value.match(
                /^\[!(NOTE|TIP|IMPORTANT|WARNING|CAUTION)\](?=$|[\t\r\n ])/,
              );
              if (!match) return;

              const alertType = match[1].toLowerCase();
              const remainingText = firstText.value
                .slice(match[0].length)
                .replace(/^[\t\r\n ]+/, "");
              const remainingChildren = [...firstParagraph.children];

              if (remainingText) {
                remainingChildren[0] = { ...firstText, value: remainingText };
              } else {
                remainingChildren.shift();
              }
              context.setProperty(
                firstParagraph,
                "children",
                remainingChildren,
              );

              const rawClasses = node.properties.className as unknown;
              const classNames = Array.isArray(rawClasses)
                ? rawClasses
                : typeof rawClasses === "string"
                  ? rawClasses.split(/\s+/)
                  : [];
              context.setProperty(node, "className", [
                ...classNames,
                "markdown-alert",
                `markdown-alert-${alertType}`,
              ]);
              context.insertBefore(firstParagraph, {
                type: "element",
                tagName: "p",
                properties: { className: ["markdown-alert-title"] },
                children: [{ type: "text", value: match[1] }],
              });

              if (remainingChildren.length === 0) {
                context.removeNode(firstParagraph);
              }
            },
          },
        },
      ],
    }),
  },
  vite: {
    plugins: [tailwindcss()],
  },
  site: siteConfig.url,
  integrations: [
    sitemap(),
    mdx(),
    icon({
      include: {
        lucide: ["*"],
      },
    }),
  ],
});

FILE: main.py
====================================================================================================

from pathlib import Path
import os

# 项目根目录：在项目根目录执行此脚本
ROOT = Path.cwd()

# 输出文件
OUTPUT = ROOT / "project_dump.txt"

# 完全排除这些文件夹（适用于任意层级）
EXCLUDED_DIRS = {
    ".git",
    "node_modules",
    "dist",
    ".astro",
    ".vscode",
    ".wrangler",
}

# 不导出这些文件
EXCLUDED_FILES = {
    OUTPUT.name,
}

SEPARATOR = "=" * 100


def scan_project():
    """扫描项目，收集目录和文件路径。"""
    directories = []
    files = []

    for current, dirs, filenames in os.walk(ROOT):
        current_path = Path(current)

        # 排除指定目录，阻止继续遍历
        dirs[:] = sorted(
            d for d in dirs
            if d not in EXCLUDED_DIRS
        )

        # 记录目录（包括空目录）
        for dirname in dirs:
            directories.append(
                (current_path / dirname).relative_to(ROOT)
            )

        # 记录文件
        for filename in sorted(filenames):
            if filename in EXCLUDED_FILES:
                continue

            path = current_path / filename

            # 避免将符号链接指向的外部文件当作项目文件
            if path.is_symlink():
                files.append(path.relative_to(ROOT))
                continue

            files.append(path.relative_to(ROOT))

    return sorted(set(directories)), sorted(set(files))


def build_tree(directories, files):
    """生成项目目录树。"""
    all_paths = set(directories) | set(files)
    children = {}

    for path in all_paths:
        parent = path.parent
        children.setdefault(parent, []).append(path)

    for parent in children:
        children[parent].sort(
            key=lambda p: (
                p not in directories,
                p.name.lower(),
            )
        )

    lines = [f"{ROOT.name}/"]

    def walk(parent, prefix=""):
        items = children.get(parent, [])

        for index, path in enumerate(items):
            is_last = index == len(items) - 1
            connector = "└── " if is_last else "├── "
            is_dir = path in directories

            name = path.name + ("/" if is_dir else "")
            lines.append(prefix + connector + name)

            if is_dir:
                next_prefix = prefix + (
                    "    " if is_last else "│   "
                )
                walk(path, next_prefix)

    walk(Path("."))
    return "\n".join(lines)


def read_text_file(path):
    """读取文本文件；二进制或非 UTF-8 文件不输出代码。"""
    try:
        if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file():
            return None, "符号链接或非普通文件"

        data = path.read_bytes()

        # 常见二进制文件特征
        if b"\x00" in data:
            return None, "二进制文件"

        try:
            content = data.decode("utf-8-sig")
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            return None, "非 UTF-8 文件，可能是二进制或其他编码"

        return content, None

    except (OSError, PermissionError) as exc:
        return None, f"读取失败：{exc}"


def export_project():
    directories, files = scan_project()

    with OUTPUT.open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as out:
        out.write("PROJECT SOURCE EXPORT\n")
        out.write(f"Project root: {ROOT}\n")
        out.write(f"Directories: {len(directories)}\n")
        out.write(f"Files: {len(files)}\n")
        out.write(f"{SEPARATOR}\n\n")

        # 第一部分：目录树
        out.write("PROJECT TREE\n")
        out.write(f"{SEPARATOR}\n")
        out.write(build_tree(directories, files))
        out.write("\n\n")

        # 第二部分：每个文件的完整内容
        out.write("FILE CONTENTS\n")
        out.write(f"{SEPARATOR}\n\n")

        skipped = []

        for relative_path in files:
            full_path = ROOT / relative_path
            content, error = read_text_file(full_path)

            out.write(f"\n{SEPARATOR}\n")
            out.write(f"FILE: {relative_path.as_posix()}\n")
            out.write(f"{SEPARATOR}\n")

            if error:
                out.write(f"[未输出内容：{error}]\n")
                skipped.append((relative_path, error))
                continue

            out.write(content)

            if not content.endswith("\n"):
                out.write("\n")

        # 第三部分：跳过文件汇总
        out.write(f"\n\n{SEPARATOR}\n")
        out.write("SKIPPED FILES SUMMARY\n")
        out.write(f"{SEPARATOR}\n")

        if skipped:
            for path, reason in skipped:
                out.write(f"{path.as_posix()} — {reason}\n")
        else:
            out.write("没有跳过任何文件。\n")

    print(f"导出完成：{OUTPUT}")
    print(f"目录数量：{len(directories)}")
    print(f"文件数量：{len(files)}")
    print(f"跳过内容的文件数量：{len(skipped)}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    export_project()

FILE: README.md
====================================================================================================
# Mare Blog

A fast, minimal personal blog built with **Astro**. Publish articles, short
journal entries, and standalone pages with a responsive reading experience,
instant search, and no server-side runtime.

## 🖼️ Preview

![Mare Blog Preview](./src/assets/preview.png)

👉 [View Live Demo →](https://mare-blog.pages.dev)

## ✨ Key Features

- **Static and fast**: Astro generates pages at build time, with no client-side JavaScript by default.
- **Full-text search**: Pagefind indexes the site during the production build.
- **Responsive themes**: Tailwind CSS styling with light and dark modes.
- **Comments**: Optional Giscus comments backed by GitHub Discussions.
- **Three content types**: Publish blog posts, timeline-style journal entries, and standalone pages.

## 🧰 Tech Stack

- [Astro](https://astro.build)
- [Tailwind CSS](https://tailwindcss.com)
- [Pagefind](https://pagefind.app)
- [Giscus](https://giscus.app)

## 🚀 Deploy to Cloudflare Pages

[![Deploy to Cloudflare Pages](https://img.shields.io/badge/Deploy%20to-Cloudflare%20Pages-F38020?logo=cloudflare&logoColor=white)](https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/pages/new/provider/github)

Open the Cloudflare Pages setup, authorize GitHub, and select your copy of this
repository. Use these build settings:

| Setting                | Value                               |
| :--------------------- | :---------------------------------- |
| Framework preset       | Astro                               |
| Build command          | `pnpm build`                        |
| Build output directory | `dist`                              |
| Environment variable   | `NODE_VERSION` = `22.12.0` or later |

The `pnpm build` command also generates the Pagefind search index. Cloudflare
Pages will build and deploy the site whenever you push changes to the connected
Git branch.

## 💻 Run Locally

Requires Node.js `22.12.0` or later and [pnpm](https://pnpm.io/).

```bash
git clone https://github.com/Niceeepoiu/mare-blog.git
cd mare-blog
pnpm install
pnpm dev
```

The development server runs at `http://localhost:4321`. Before publishing,
update [`site.config.ts`](./site.config.ts) with your site details and
integrations.

## 📖 Documentation

See the [configuration and usage guide](https://mare-blog.pages.dev/blog/guide/)
for site settings, Giscus setup, and instructions for publishing content.

## 🧞 Commands

| Command                | Action                                         |
| :--------------------- | :--------------------------------------------- |
| `pnpm install`         | Installs dependencies                          |
| `pnpm dev`             | Starts local dev server at `localhost:4321`    |
| `pnpm build`           | Builds your production site to `./dist/`       |
| `pnpm preview`         | Previews your production build locally         |
| `pnpm format`          | Formats code across the project using Prettier |
| `pnpm astro ...`       | Runs Astro CLI commands                        |
| `pnpm astro -- --help` | Gets help using the Astro CLI                  |

export interface NavItem {
  /** Navigation item display label */
  name: string;
  /** Navigation target path or URL */
  href: string;
  /** Icon identifier */
  icon?: string;
}

export interface AuthorLink {
  /** Link display label */
  name: string;
  /** Link target URL */
  href: string;
  /** Icon identifier */
  icon?: string;
}

export interface SiteConfig {
  /** Site language */
  lang: string;
  /** Site title */
  title: string;
  /** Site description */
  description: string;
  /** Author name */
  author: string;
  /** Author URL */
  authorUrl: string;
  /** Author biography */
  authorBio: string;
  /** Detailed author introduction displayed on the About page */
  authorDetails: string;
  /** Author location */
  authorLocation: string;
  /** GitHub username used for the contribution graph */
  githubUsername: string;
  /** Author avatar image URL */
  avatar: string;
  /** Optional array of author links */
  authorLinks?: AuthorLink[];
  /** Base URL of the site */
  url: string;
  /** Number of posts to show on each blog archive page */
  postsPerPage: number;
  /** Header navigation items */
  nav: NavItem[];
  /** Giscus comment system configuration */
  giscus: {
    /** Enable or disable Giscus comments */
    enabled: boolean;
    /** Target GitHub repository in 'owner/repo' format */
    repo: string;
    /** Repository GraphQL Node ID */
    repoId: string;
    /** Discussion category name */
    category: string;
    /** Discussion category GraphQL Node ID */
    categoryId: string;
    /** Theme for light mode */
    theme: string;
    /** Theme for dark mode */
    darkTheme: string;
    /** UI language */
    lang: string;
  };
}

export function defineConfig(config: SiteConfig): SiteConfig {
  return config;
}

export default defineConfig({

FILE: src/components/AboutProfileCard.astro

---
import siteConfig from "@config";
import { Icon } from "astro-icon/components";
import type { GitHubContributionDay } from "../utils/github-contributions";

interface Props {
  contributions: GitHubContributionDay[];
  contributionError?: string;
}

const { contributions, contributionError } = Astro.props;
const millisecondsPerWeek = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
const firstContributionDate = contributions[0]
  ? new Date(`${contributions[0].date}T00:00:00Z`)
  : undefined;
const weekCount = firstContributionDate
  ? Math.floor(
      (new Date(
        `${contributions[contributions.length - 1].date}T00:00:00Z`,
      ).getTime() -
        firstContributionDate.getTime()) /
        millisecondsPerWeek,
    ) + 1
  : 0;
let previousMonth: number | undefined;
const monthFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en", {
  month: "short",
  timeZone: "UTC",
});
const weeks = Array.from({ length: weekCount }, (_, index) => {
  const date = new Date(
    firstContributionDate!.getTime() + index * millisecondsPerWeek,
  );
  const month = date.getUTCMonth();
  const label = month === previousMonth ? "" : monthFormatter.format(date);
  previousMonth = month;
  return { index, label };
}).filter((week) => week.label);
const monthLabels = weeks.map((week, index) => ({
  ...week,
  end: weeks[index + 1]?.index ?? weekCount,
}));
const contributionLevels = [
  "bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800",
  "bg-green-200 dark:bg-green-950",
  "bg-green-300 dark:bg-green-800",
  "bg-green-500 dark:bg-green-600",
  "bg-green-700 dark:bg-green-400",
];
const totalContributions = contributions.reduce(
  (total, day) => total + day.count,
  0,
);
---

<section
  aria-label="Author profile"
  class="mb-8 overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-white shadow-sm dark:bg-gray-900"
>
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-6 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:p-8">
    <img
      src={siteConfig.avatar}
      alt={`${siteConfig.author}'s avatar`}
      loading="lazy"
      width="112"
      height="112"
      class="h-24 w-24 shrink-0 rounded-full object-cover sm:h-28 sm:w-28"
    />
    <div class="min-w-0">
      <p class="m-0 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-3xl">
        {siteConfig.author}
      </p>
      <div class="mt-2 mb-3 space-y-3 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">
        {
          siteConfig.authorDetails
            .split(/\n\s*\n/)
            .map((paragraph) => paragraph.trim())
            .filter(Boolean)
            .map((paragraph) => <p class="m-0 leading-relaxed">{paragraph}</p>)
        }
      </div>
      <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <span
          class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400"
        >
          <Icon name="lucide:map-pin" class="h-4 w-4" />
          {siteConfig.authorLocation}
        </span>
        {
          siteConfig.authorLinks?.map((link) => (
            <a
              href={link.href}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              aria-label={link.name}
              class="nav-scale-bg m-2.5 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 no-underline transition-colors duration-200 hover:text-blue-600 hover:before:bg-blue-50/70 dark:text-gray-400 dark:hover:text-blue-400 dark:hover:before:bg-gray-800"
            >
              {link.icon && <Icon name={link.icon} class="h-4 w-4" />}
              <span>{link.name}</span>
            </a>
          ))
        }
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div
    class="border-t border-gray-200 px-3 py-4 sm:px-8 sm:py-5 dark:border-gray-800"
  >
    <div class="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Icon
          name="lucide:github"
          class="h-5 w-5 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400"
        />
        <h3 class="m-0 text-base font-semibold">GitHub contributions</h3>
        <span class="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
          @{siteConfig.githubUsername}
        </span>
      </div>
      {
        contributions.length > 0 && (
          <span class="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
            <b>{totalContributions}</b> contributions in the last year
          </span>
        )
      }
    </div>
    {
      contributionError ? (
        <p
          role="status"
          class="m-0 rounded-md bg-amber-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-200"
        >
          {contributionError}
        </p>
      ) : contributions.length > 0 ? (
        <div class="min-w-0">
          <div
            aria-hidden="true"
            class="mb-2 grid gap-px text-[0.65rem] text-gray-500 sm:ml-8 sm:text-xs dark:text-gray-400"
            style={`grid-template-columns: repeat(${weekCount}, minmax(0, 1fr))`}
          >
            {monthLabels.map((month) => (
              <span
                class="overflow-visible text-center whitespace-nowrap"
                style={`grid-column: ${month.index + 1} / ${month.end + 1}`}
              >
                {month.label}
              </span>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-[1.5rem_minmax(0,1fr)] sm:gap-x-2">
            <div
              aria-hidden="true"
              class="hidden grid-rows-7 gap-px text-[0.65rem] text-gray-500 sm:grid dark:text-gray-400"
            >
              <span class="row-start-2">Mon</span>
              <span class="row-start-4">Wed</span>
              <span class="row-start-6">Fri</span>
            </div>
            <div
              role="img"
              aria-label={`${totalContributions} GitHub contributions by ${siteConfig.githubUsername} in the last year`}
              class="grid min-w-0 gap-px"
              style={`grid-template-columns: repeat(${weekCount}, minmax(0, 1fr)); grid-template-rows: repeat(7, minmax(0, 1fr))`}
            >
              {contributions.map((day) => {
                const date = new Date(`${day.date}T00:00:00Z`);
                const week = Math.floor(
                  (date.getTime() - firstContributionDate!.getTime()) /
                    millisecondsPerWeek,
                );
                const weekday = date.getUTCDay();
                const formattedDate = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en", {
                  dateStyle: "long",
                  timeZone: "UTC",
                }).format(date);
                const contributionLabel =
                  day.count === 1 ? "contribution" : "contributions";

                return (
                  <span
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    data-tooltip={`${day.count} ${contributionLabel} on ${formattedDate}`}
                    class={`aspect-square rounded-xs ${contributionLevels[day.level]} hover:ring-1 hover:ring-gray-400 dark:hover:ring-gray-500`}
                    style={`grid-column: ${week + 1}; grid-row: ${weekday + 1}`}
                  />
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
            <a
              href="https://docs.github.com/articles/why-are-my-contributions-not-showing-up-on-my-profile"
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              class="hover:text-blue-600 hover:underline dark:hover:text-blue-400"
            >
              Learn how we count contributions
            </a>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span>Less</span>
              {contributionLevels.map((level) => (
                <span class={`h-3 w-3 rounded-xs ${level}`} />
              ))}
              <span>More</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p role="status" class="m-0 text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
          No contribution data is available yet.
        </p>
      )
    }
  </div>
</section>

import { Icon } from "astro-icon/components";
---

<button
  id="back-to-top"
  aria-label="Back to top"
  data-tooltip="Back to top"
  class="pointer-events-none fixed right-6 bottom-6 z-50 translate-y-4 cursor-pointer rounded-full border border-zinc-200 bg-white/80 p-3 text-zinc-700 opacity-0 shadow-lg backdrop-blur-md transition-all duration-300 hover:scale-110 focus:outline-none active:scale-95 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 dark:text-zinc-200"
>
  <Icon name="lucide:arrow-up" class="h-5 w-5" />
</button>

<script>
  type BackToTopWindow = Window & {
    __backToTopCleanup?: () => void;
  };

  const typedWindow = window as BackToTopWindow;

  const setupBackToTop = () => {
    if (typedWindow.__backToTopCleanup) {
      typedWindow.__backToTopCleanup();
    }

    const btn = document.getElementById("back-to-top");
    if (!btn) return;

    const toggleVisibility = () => {
      if (window.scrollY > 300) {
        btn.classList.remove(
          "opacity-0",
          "pointer-events-none",
          "translate-y-4",
        );
        btn.classList.add(
          "opacity-100",
          "pointer-events-auto",
          "translate-y-0",
        );
      } else {
        btn.classList.remove(
          "opacity-100",
          "pointer-events-auto",
          "translate-y-0",
        );
        btn.classList.add("opacity-0", "pointer-events-none", "translate-y-4");
      }
    };

    const scrollToTop = () => {
      window.scrollTo({
        top: 0,
        behavior: "smooth",
      });

      btn.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent("mouseout", { bubbles: true }));
    };

    window.addEventListener("scroll", toggleVisibility, { passive: true });
    btn.addEventListener("click", scrollToTop);

    typedWindow.__backToTopCleanup = () => {
      window.removeEventListener("scroll", toggleVisibility);
      btn.removeEventListener("click", scrollToTop);
    };

    toggleVisibility();
  };

  const cleanupBackToTop = () => {
    if (typedWindow.__backToTopCleanup) {
      typedWindow.__backToTopCleanup();
      delete typedWindow.__backToTopCleanup;
    }
  };

  document.addEventListener("astro:before-swap", cleanupBackToTop);
  document.addEventListener("astro:page-load", setupBackToTop);
  setupBackToTop();
</script>

{
  shouldRenderComments && (
    <section
      id="comments-section"
      data-repo={giscus.repo}
      data-repo-id={giscus.repoId}
      data-category={giscus.category}
      data-category-id={giscus.categoryId}
      data-theme={giscus.theme}
      data-dark-theme={giscus.darkTheme}
      data-lang={giscus.lang}
      class="w-full rounded-lg bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-8 lg:p-12 dark:bg-gray-900"
    >
      <div
        data-comments-loading
        class="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-lg text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400"
        role="status"
        aria-live="polite"

        <span class="h-4 w-4 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-gray-300 border-t-gray-600 dark:border-gray-700 dark:border-t-gray-300" />

        class="hidden text-center text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400"
        role="alert"

        <p>Comments could not be loaded. Please try again later.</p>
        <button
          data-comments-retry
          type="button"
          class="mt-3 inline-flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 transition hover:bg-gray-100 active:scale-98 dark:border-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 dark:hover:bg-gray-800"
        >

          <span>Refresh comments</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      <div data-comments-mount />
    </section>
  )
}

<script is:inline>
  const COMMENTS_LOAD_TIMEOUT = 10000;

  const cleanupCommentsState = () => {
    if (window.__commentsCleanup) {
      window.__commentsCleanup();
      delete window.__commentsCleanup;
    }

    if (window.__giscusMessageHandler) {
      window.removeEventListener("message", window.__giscusMessageHandler);
      delete window.__giscusMessageHandler;
    }

    if (document.__giscusThemeHandler) {
      document.removeEventListener(
        "theme-changed",
        document.__giscusThemeHandler,
      );
      delete document.__giscusThemeHandler;
    }
  };

  const setupComments = () => {
    const section = document.getElementById("comments-section");
    if (!(section instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

    const mount = section.querySelector("[data-comments-mount]");
    const loading = section.querySelector("[data-comments-loading]");
    const error = section.querySelector("[data-comments-error]");
    const retry = section.querySelector("[data-comments-retry]");

    if (
      !(mount instanceof HTMLElement) ||
      !(loading instanceof HTMLElement) ||
      !(error instanceof HTMLElement) ||
      !(retry instanceof HTMLButtonElement)
    ) {
      return;
    }

    if (section.dataset.commentsInitialized === "true") return;
    section.dataset.commentsInitialized = "true";

    let loadTimeout;
    let observer;

    const setState = (state) => {
      const isLoading = state === "loading";
      const isError = state === "error";

      loading.classList.toggle("hidden", !isLoading);
      error.classList.toggle("hidden", !isError);
      mount.classList.toggle("hidden", isLoading || isError);
    };

    const stopWatching = () => {
      window.clearTimeout(loadTimeout);
      observer?.disconnect();
      observer = undefined;
    };

    const showError = () => {
      stopWatching();
      mount.replaceChildren();
      setState("error");
    };

    const markLoaded = () => {
      const iframe = mount.querySelector("iframe.giscus-frame");
      if (!(iframe instanceof HTMLIFrameElement)) return;

      stopWatching();
      setState("loaded");
      updateGiscusTheme();
    };

    const loadComments = () => {
      stopWatching();
      mount.replaceChildren();
      setState("loading");

      const script = document.createElement("script");
      script.src = "https://giscus.app/client.js";
      script.async = true;
      script.crossOrigin = "anonymous";
      script.dataset.giscusComments = "true";
      script.dataset.repo = section.dataset.repo || "";
      script.dataset.repoId = section.dataset.repoId || "";
      script.dataset.category = section.dataset.category || "";
      script.dataset.categoryId = section.dataset.categoryId || "";
      script.dataset.mapping = "pathname";
      script.dataset.reactionsEnabled = "1";
      script.dataset.inputPosition = "top";
      script.dataset.theme = section.dataset.theme || "light";
      script.dataset.lang = section.dataset.lang || "en";

      script.addEventListener("error", showError, { once: true });
      script.addEventListener(
        "load",
        () => {
          observer = new MutationObserver(markLoaded);
          observer.observe(mount, { childList: true, subtree: true });
          markLoaded();
        },
        { once: true },
      );

      mount.append(script);
      loadTimeout = window.setTimeout(showError, COMMENTS_LOAD_TIMEOUT);
    };

    retry.addEventListener("click", loadComments);
    loadComments();

    window.__commentsCleanup = () => {
      section.dataset.commentsInitialized = "false";
      stopWatching();
      retry.removeEventListener("click", loadComments);
      mount.replaceChildren();
    };
  };

  const updateGiscusTheme = () => {
    const section = document.getElementById("comments-section");
    if (!section) return;

    const light = section.dataset.theme || "light";
    const dark = section.dataset.darkTheme || "dark";
    const targetTheme = document.documentElement.classList.contains("dark")
      ? dark
      : light;
    const iframe = section.querySelector("iframe.giscus-frame");

    if (!(iframe instanceof HTMLIFrameElement)) return;

    iframe.contentWindow?.postMessage(
      {
        giscus: {
          setConfig: {
            theme: targetTheme,
          },
        },
      },
      "https://giscus.app",
    );
  };

  if (!window.__giscusMessageHandler) {
    window.__giscusMessageHandler = (event) => {
      if (event.origin !== "https://giscus.app") return;
      if (!(typeof event.data === "object" && event.data.giscus)) return;
      updateGiscusTheme();
    };
    window.addEventListener("message", window.__giscusMessageHandler);
  }

  if (!document.__giscusThemeHandler) {
    document.__giscusThemeHandler = () => updateGiscusTheme();
    document.addEventListener("theme-changed", document.__giscusThemeHandler);
  }

  document.addEventListener("astro:before-swap", cleanupCommentsState);
  setupComments();
  document.addEventListener("astro:page-load", setupComments);
</script>

FILE: src/components/GlobalTooltip.astro
====================================================================================================

---

<script>
  type GlobalTooltipWindow = Window & {
    __globalTooltipCleanup?: () => void;
  };

  const typedWindow = window as GlobalTooltipWindow;

  const setupGlobalTooltip = () => {
    if (typedWindow.__globalTooltipCleanup) {
      typedWindow.__globalTooltipCleanup();
    }

    let currentTooltipEl: HTMLElement | null = null;
    let activeTarget: HTMLElement | null = null;

    const BASE_CLASSES = [
      "fixed",
      "z-[100]",
      "pointer-events-none",
      "whitespace-nowrap",
      "px-2.5",
      "py-1",
      "text-xs",
      "font-medium",
      "rounded-md",
      "shadow-sm",
      "border",
      "backdrop-blur-sm",
      "transition-opacity",
      "duration-150",
      "ease-in-out",
      "opacity-0",
      "-translate-x-1/2",
      "top-[var(--tooltip-top)]",
      "left-[var(--tooltip-left)]",
      "text-gray-100",
      "bg-gray-900/90",
      "border-gray-700/50",
      "dark:text-gray-900",
      "dark:bg-gray-100/90",
      "dark:border-gray-300/50",
    ];

    const removeTooltip = () => {
      if (currentTooltipEl) {
        currentTooltipEl.classList.remove("opacity-100");
        currentTooltipEl.classList.add("opacity-0");
        const elToRemove = currentTooltipEl;
        currentTooltipEl = null;
        activeTarget = null;
        setTimeout(() => {
          elToRemove.remove();
        }, 150);
      }
    };

    const updatePosition = (target: HTMLElement, tooltip: HTMLElement) => {
      const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
      const viewportWidth = window.innerWidth;

      let left = rect.left + rect.width / 2;

      const padding = 12;
      const tooltipWidth = tooltip.offsetWidth || 120;
      const halfWidth = tooltipWidth / 2;

      if (left - halfWidth < padding) {
        left = padding + halfWidth;
      } else if (left + halfWidth > viewportWidth - padding) {
        left = viewportWidth - padding - halfWidth;
      }

      if (rect.top < 60) {
        tooltip.classList.remove("-translate-y-full");
        tooltip.classList.add("translate-y-0");
        tooltip.style.setProperty("--tooltip-top", `${rect.bottom + 8}px`);
      } else {
        tooltip.classList.remove("translate-y-0");
        tooltip.classList.add("-translate-y-full");
        tooltip.style.setProperty("--tooltip-top", `${rect.top - 8}px`);
      }

      tooltip.style.setProperty("--tooltip-left", `${left}px`);
    };

    const handleMouseOver = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      const target = (event.target as HTMLElement).closest(
        "[data-tooltip]",
      ) as HTMLElement;

      if (!target || target === activeTarget) return;

      const text = target.getAttribute("data-tooltip");
      if (!text) return;

      if (currentTooltipEl) {
        currentTooltipEl.remove();
        currentTooltipEl = null;
      }

      activeTarget = target;

      const tooltip = document.createElement("div");

      const tooltipId = `tooltip-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 9)}`;
      tooltip.id = tooltipId;
      tooltip.setAttribute("role", "tooltip");
      target.setAttribute("aria-describedby", tooltipId);

      tooltip.classList.add(...BASE_CLASSES);
      tooltip.textContent = text;

      document.body.appendChild(tooltip);
      currentTooltipEl = tooltip;

      updatePosition(target, tooltip);

      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
          if (currentTooltipEl === tooltip) {
            tooltip.classList.remove("opacity-0");
            tooltip.classList.add("opacity-100");
          }
        });
      });
    };

    const handleMouseOut = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (!activeTarget) return;

      const target = (event.target as HTMLElement).closest("[data-tooltip]");
      if (target === activeTarget) {
        const relatedTarget = event.relatedTarget as Node | null;
        if (!relatedTarget || !target.contains(relatedTarget)) {
          activeTarget.removeAttribute("aria-describedby");
          removeTooltip();
        }
      }
    };

    const handleScroll = () => {
      if (activeTarget && currentTooltipEl) {
        updatePosition(activeTarget, currentTooltipEl);
      }
    };

    const handleKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape" && currentTooltipEl) {
        if (activeTarget) activeTarget.removeAttribute("aria-describedby");
        removeTooltip();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener("mouseover", handleMouseOver);
    document.addEventListener("mouseout", handleMouseOut);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    window.addEventListener("scroll", handleScroll, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener("resize", handleScroll, { passive: true });

    typedWindow.__globalTooltipCleanup = () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mouseover", handleMouseOver);
      document.removeEventListener("mouseout", handleMouseOut);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
      window.removeEventListener("scroll", handleScroll);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", handleScroll);
      if (activeTarget) {
        activeTarget.removeAttribute("aria-describedby");
      }
      if (currentTooltipEl) {
        currentTooltipEl.remove();
        currentTooltipEl = null;
      }
      activeTarget = null;
    };
  };

  const cleanupGlobalTooltip = () => {
    if (typedWindow.__globalTooltipCleanup) {
      typedWindow.__globalTooltipCleanup();
      delete typedWindow.__globalTooltipCleanup;
    }
  };

  document.addEventListener("astro:before-swap", cleanupGlobalTooltip);
  document.addEventListener("astro:page-load", setupGlobalTooltip);
  setupGlobalTooltip();
</script>

import siteConfig from "@config";
import ThemeToggle from "./ThemeToggle.astro";
import Search from "./Search.astro";
import { Icon } from "astro-icon/components";

const currentPath = Astro.url.pathname.replace(/\/$/, "") || "/";
const navItems = siteConfig.nav.map((item) => {
  const normalizedHref = item.href.replace(/\/$/, "") || "/";
  return {
    ...item,
    normalizedHref,
  };
});

const activeNavHref = navItems
  .map((item) => item.normalizedHref)
  .filter((href) =>
    href === "/"
      ? currentPath === "/"
      : currentPath === href || currentPath.startsWith(`${href}/`),
  )
  .sort((left, right) => right.length - left.length)[0];
---

<header
  class="mx-4 my-8 flex max-w-5xl flex-col rounded-lg bg-white shadow-sm sm:mx-6 lg:mx-auto dark:bg-gray-900"

                isActive
                  ? "nav-active bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 dark:bg-blue-950/60 dark:text-blue-400"

              <span>{item.name}</span>
            </a>
          );

<script>
  type HeaderWindow = Window & {
    __headerCleanup?: () => void;
  };

  const typedWindow = window as HeaderWindow;

  const closeMobileNavigation = () => {
    const menu = document.getElementById("mobile-navigation-menu");
    const toggle = document.getElementById("mobile-navigation-toggle");
    if (!menu || !toggle || menu.hidden) return;

    toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");

    const prefersReducedMotion = window.matchMedia(
      "(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)",
    ).matches;
    if (prefersReducedMotion) {
      menu.hidden = true;
      return;
    }

    menu.classList.add("is-closing");

    const handleAnimationEnd = () => {
      menu.classList.remove("is-closing");
      menu.hidden = true;
      menu.removeEventListener("animationend", handleAnimationEnd);
    };

    menu.addEventListener("animationend", handleAnimationEnd, { once: true });
  };

  const openMobileNavigation = () => {
    const menu = document.getElementById("mobile-navigation-menu");
    const toggle = document.getElementById("mobile-navigation-toggle");
    if (!menu || !toggle) return;

    menu.classList.remove("is-closing");
    menu.hidden = false;
    toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
  };

  const setupHeaderInteractions = () => {
    if (typedWindow.__headerCleanup) {
      typedWindow.__headerCleanup();
      delete typedWindow.__headerCleanup;
    }

    const handleDocumentClick = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (!(event.target instanceof Element)) return;

      const menu = document.getElementById("mobile-navigation-menu");
      const toggle = event.target.closest("#mobile-navigation-toggle");

      if (toggle && menu) {
        const isOpen = !menu.hidden && !menu.classList.contains("is-closing");
        if (isOpen) {
          closeMobileNavigation();
        } else {
          openMobileNavigation();
        }
        return;
      }

      if (menu && !menu.hidden && !menu.classList.contains("is-closing")) {
        const clickedLink = event.target.closest("#mobile-navigation-menu a");
        const clickedOutside = !menu.contains(event.target);
        if (clickedLink || clickedOutside) closeMobileNavigation();
      }
    };

    const handleKeydown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key !== "Escape") return;

      const menu = document.getElementById("mobile-navigation-menu");
      const toggle = document.getElementById("mobile-navigation-toggle");
      if (!menu || menu.hidden) return;

      closeMobileNavigation();
      toggle?.focus();
    };

    document.addEventListener("click", handleDocumentClick);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeydown);

    typedWindow.__headerCleanup = () => {
      document.removeEventListener("click", handleDocumentClick);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeydown);
    };
  };

  document.addEventListener("astro:before-swap", () => {
    if (typedWindow.__headerCleanup) {
      typedWindow.__headerCleanup();
      delete typedWindow.__headerCleanup;
    }
  });

  setupHeaderInteractions();
  document.addEventListener("astro:page-load", setupHeaderInteractions);
</script>

<style>
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) {
    #mobile-navigation-menu:not([hidden]) {
      animation: mobile-navigation-enter 180ms ease-out both;
    }

    #mobile-navigation-menu.is-closing {
      animation: mobile-navigation-exit 150ms ease-in both;
    }

    @keyframes mobile-navigation-enter {
      from {
        opacity: 0;
        transform: translateY(-0.5rem);
      }
      to {
        opacity: 1;
        transform: translateY(0);
      }
    }

    @keyframes mobile-navigation-exit {
      from {
        opacity: 1;
        transform: translateY(0);
      }
      to {
        opacity: 0;
        transform: translateY(-0.5rem);
      }
    }
  }
</style>

  @import "@pagefind/component-ui/css";

<script is:inline>
  const syncPagefindTheme = () => {
    const pagefindModal = document.querySelector("pagefind-modal");
    if (!(pagefindModal instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

    if (document.documentElement.classList.contains("dark")) {
      pagefindModal.setAttribute("data-pf-theme", "dark");
    } else {
      pagefindModal.removeAttribute("data-pf-theme");
    }
  };

  const setupSearchToggle = () => {
    syncPagefindTheme();

    const searchButton = document.getElementById("search-toggle");
    const devDialog = document.getElementById("search-dev-dialog");

    if (!searchButton) return;

    if (devDialog instanceof HTMLDialogElement) {
      devDialog.onclick = (event) => {
        if (event.target === devDialog) devDialog.close();
      };
    }

    searchButton.onclick = () => {
      if (devDialog instanceof HTMLDialogElement) {
        if (!devDialog.open) devDialog.showModal();
        return;
      }

      const modal = document.querySelector("pagefind-modal");
      if (modal && "open" in modal && typeof modal.open === "function") {
        modal.open();
      }
    };
  };

  setupSearchToggle();
  document.addEventListener("astro:page-load", setupSearchToggle);
  document.addEventListener("theme-changed", syncPagefindTheme);
  document.addEventListener("astro:after-swap", syncPagefindTheme);
</script>

FILE: src/components/ThemeToggle.astro
====================================================================================================
---
import { Icon } from "astro-icon/components";
---

<button
  id="theme-toggle"
  aria-label="Toggle theme"
  data-tooltip="Toggle theme"
  class="nav-scale-bg cursor-pointer rounded-md p-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 transition-colors duration-200 hover:text-blue-600 hover:before:bg-blue-50/70 dark:text-gray-400 dark:hover:text-blue-400 dark:hover:before:bg-gray-800"
>
  <span id="theme-toggle-light-icon" class="hidden">
    <Icon name="lucide:sun" class="h-5 w-5" />
  </span>

  <span id="theme-toggle-dark-icon" class="hidden">
    <Icon name="lucide:moon" class="h-5 w-5" />
  </span>
</button>

<script is:inline>
  const updateIcon = (isDark) => {
    const darkIcon = document.getElementById("theme-toggle-dark-icon");
    const lightIcon = document.getElementById("theme-toggle-light-icon");
    if (!darkIcon || !lightIcon) return;

    if (isDark) {
      lightIcon.classList.remove("hidden");
      darkIcon.classList.add("hidden");
    } else {
      darkIcon.classList.remove("hidden");
      lightIcon.classList.add("hidden");
    }
  };

  const setupThemeToggle = () => {
    const toggleBtn = document.getElementById("theme-toggle");
    if (!toggleBtn) return;

    const initialIsDark = document.documentElement.classList.contains("dark");
    updateIcon(initialIsDark);

    toggleBtn.onclick = (event) => {
      const isDark = document.documentElement.classList.contains("dark");
      const nextIsDark = !isDark;

      const toggleTheme = () => {
        document.documentElement.classList.toggle("dark", nextIsDark);
        localStorage.setItem("theme", nextIsDark ? "dark" : "light");
        updateIcon(nextIsDark);

        document.dispatchEvent(
          new CustomEvent("theme-changed", { detail: { isDark: nextIsDark } }),
        );
      };

      const isAppearanceTransition =
        document.startViewTransition &&
        !window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

      if (!isAppearanceTransition) {
        toggleTheme();
        return;
      }

      const rect = toggleBtn.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x = event.clientX ?? rect.left + rect.width / 2;
      const y = event.clientY ?? rect.top + rect.height / 2;

      const endRadius = Math.hypot(
        Math.max(x, innerWidth - x),
        Math.max(y, innerHeight - y),
      );

      const transition = document.startViewTransition(() => {
        toggleTheme();
      });

      transition.ready.then(() => {
        const clipPath = [
          `circle(0px at ${x}px ${y}px)`,
          `circle(${endRadius}px at ${x}px ${y}px)`,
        ];

        document.documentElement.animate(
          {
            clipPath: clipPath,
          },
          {
            duration: 450,
            easing: "cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)",
            pseudoElement: "::view-transition-new(root)",
          },
        );
      });
    };
  };

  setupThemeToggle();
  document.addEventListener("astro:after-swap", setupThemeToggle);
</script>

---
title: "GitHub-Style Alerts in Markdown"
description: "A standalone showcase of the five GitHub-style alert blockquotes supported on this blog."
pubDate: 2026-10-04
featured: false
tags: [markdown, github]
---

GitHub-style alerts turn familiar blockquotes into visual callouts. Start a quote with an alert marker to call attention to useful context, a helpful tip, an important detail, a warning, or a caution.

## Note

Use a note for useful context that helps readers understand what follows.

```markdown
> [!NOTE]
> Alert markers are case-sensitive and go at the start of the first line inside the quote.
```

> [!NOTE]
> Alert markers are case-sensitive and go at the start of the first line inside the quote.

## Tip

Use a tip to share an optional shortcut or a more convenient approach.

```markdown
> [!TIP]
> Keep each alert focused on one idea so readers can scan it quickly.
```

> [!TIP]
> Keep each alert focused on one idea so readers can scan it quickly.

## Important

Use an important alert for information readers should not miss.

```markdown
> [!IMPORTANT]
> This blog supports all five GitHub-style alert types in Markdown posts.
```

> [!IMPORTANT]
> This blog supports all five GitHub-style alert types in Markdown posts.

## Warning

Use a warning when an action may have an unexpected effect.

```markdown
> [!WARNING]
> Changing a post's slug changes its public URL. Update any links that point to the old address.
```

> [!WARNING]
> Changing a post's slug changes its public URL. Update any links that point to the old address.

## Caution

Use a caution to call out a possible downside or consequence.

```markdown
> [!CAUTION]
> Alerts emphasize a small amount of key information; they are not a replacement for clear headings and body text.
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Alerts emphasize a small amount of key information; they are not a replacement for clear headings and body text.

FILE: src/content/blog/guide.md
====================================================================================================
---
title: "Getting Started"
description: "A comprehensive guide on configuring your site, managing pages, publishing posts, and executing CLI commands in Mare Blog."
pubDate: 2026-08-30
featured: false
tags: [astro, blogging]
---

## 1. Deploying to Cloudflare Pages

You can deploy your own copy of Mare Blog using Cloudflare Pages' Git integration:

[![Deploy to Cloudflare Pages](https://img.shields.io/badge/Deploy%20to-Cloudflare%20Pages-F38020?logo=cloudflare&logoColor=white)](https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/pages/new/provider/github)

Sign in to Cloudflare, connect GitHub, and select your fork or copy of the
repository. Configure the project with:

| Setting                | Value        |
| :--------------------- | :----------- |
| Framework preset       | Astro        |
| Build command          | `pnpm build` |
| Build output directory | `dist`       |

The build command runs Astro and creates the Pagefind search index. Once the
first deployment succeeds, pushes to the connected branch trigger new builds.
After deployment, update `url` in `site.config.ts` to your site's public URL.

## 2. Configuring Your Site

All global site metadata and third-party integrations are managed in
`site.config.ts`.

This project ships with a working default configuration that you can customize to fit your site:

```typescript
export default defineConfig({
  lang: "en",
  title: "Mare Blog",
  description: "A minimal, lightning-fast personal blog built with Astro.",
  author: "Niceeepoiu",
  authorUrl: "https://www.github.com/Niceeepoiu",
  authorBio: "A passionate developer.",
  authorDetails:
    "A student with basic full-stack development skills who enjoys playing Minecraft.",
  authorLocation: "Zhejiang, China",
  githubUsername: "Niceeepoiu",
  avatar: "/avatar.webp",
  authorLinks: [
    {
      name: "GitHub",
      href: "https://www.github.com/Niceeepoiu",
      icon: "lucide:github",
    },
  ],
  url: "https://mare-blog.pages.dev",
  postsPerPage: 6,
  nav: [
    { name: "Home", href: "/", icon: "lucide:home" },
    { name: "Blog", href: "/blog", icon: "lucide:book-open" },
    { name: "Journal", href: "/journal", icon: "lucide:calendar-days" },
    { name: "About", href: "/about", icon: "lucide:user" },
  ],
  giscus: {
    enabled: true,
    repo: "Niceeepoiu/mare-blog",
    repoId: "R_kgDOUH3Ucg",
    category: "Announcements",
    categoryId: "DIC_kwDOUH3Ucs4DEfws",
    theme: "light",
    darkTheme: "dark",
    lang: "en",
  },
});
```

The project defaults to Giscus enabled, but comments only render if the repository, repository ID, category, and category ID are all valid.

### Configuration Options Breakdown

#### Main Site Metadata

- `lang`: The site's language code.
- `title`: The global title of your blog used in SEO meta tags and the header.
- `description`: Default meta description used for social sharing and search engines.
- `author`: The name displayed in post footers and other author metadata.
- `authorUrl`: External link to your GitHub, personal website, or portfolio.
- `authorBio`: Short introduction used in author-related UI blocks.
- `authorDetails`: Detailed introduction displayed on the About page profile card. Separate paragraphs with a blank line.
- `authorLocation`: Optional location text displayed with the author profile.
- `githubUsername`: GitHub username used to display the contribution graph on the About page.
- `avatar`: URL for the author avatar image.
- `authorLinks`: Optional list of profile links such as GitHub, X, or LinkedIn.
- `url`: The public base URL of the site.
- `postsPerPage`: Number of posts displayed on each blog archive page.
- `nav`: Array of header links. Each item requires `name` and `href`; `icon` is optional and uses identifiers like `lucide:home`.

#### Giscus Comment System Settings

- `enabled`: Toggle `true` or `false` to show or hide comments globally.
- `repo`: GitHub repository in `owner/repo` format.
- `repoId`: Repository GraphQL node ID generated by giscus.
- `category`: The discussion category name to use for comments.
- `categoryId`: Category GraphQL node ID generated by giscus.
- `theme`: Theme name used in light mode.
- `darkTheme`: Theme name used in dark mode.
- `lang`: Language code used by the comment widget UI.

### Step-by-Step Guide: How to Obtain Giscus Credentials

Follow these steps to generate your required `repoId` and `categoryId`:

1. **Prepare Your GitHub Repository**:
   - Ensure your repository is **public** (Giscus does not work with private repositories).
   - Enable the **Discussions** feature in the repository settings.

2. **Install the Giscus App**:
   - Grant Giscus permission to access your repository by visiting [github.com/apps/giscus](https://github.com/apps/giscus).

3. **Generate Credentials via the Giscus Website**:
   - Go to [giscus.app](https://giscus.app).
   - Enter your repository path in the **Repository** field.
   - Choose a suitable discussion category.
   - Copy the generated `data-repo-id` and `data-category-id` values.
   - Paste them into `/site.config.ts` and keep `enabled: true`.

To customize the site, update the fields in `site.config.ts`. You can change
the content and structure independently.

## 3. Modifying the About Page

The About page profile card uses `authorDetails` in `site.config.ts`; its
Markdown/MDX page content is rendered by `src/pages/about.astro` from
`src/content/page/about.mdx`.

To update your personal bio:

1. Update `authorDetails` in `site.config.ts` to edit the profile card introduction.
2. Open the About MDX file to edit the page content.
3. Update the Frontmatter metadata at the top if needed.
4. Edit the content below the Frontmatter to update the page copy, project showcase, or links. Since MDX is enabled, you can also embed Astro or JSX components seamlessly.

```markdown
---
title: "About"
description: "Learn more about me and this blog."
---

Write your Markdown or MDX content here.
```

## 4. Writing & Publishing Posts

Blog articles live inside the content directory: `src/content/blog/`.

To publish a new article:

1. Create a new `.md` or `.mdx` file in `src/content/blog/` (e.g., `my-first-post.md`).
2. Add the required Frontmatter metadata at the top of the file. Set `featured` to
   `true` if the post should appear in the homepage's featured section; it defaults
   to `false` when omitted.

```markdown
---
title: "Blog Title"
description: "A short description of the post..."
pubDate: 2026-08-30
featured: true
tags: [astro, writing]
---

Write your Markdown or MDX content here.
```

Every new post appears in the blog list. Only posts with `featured: true` appear
in the homepage's featured section. If no posts are featured, the section shows
an empty-state message.

Use the optional `tags` field to group related posts. Tags appear on article
cards and article pages, link to their tag archive, and are listed at
`/blog/tags`. Posts without tags remain valid and are omitted from the tag index.

```yaml
tags:
  - astro
  - writing
```

## 5. Managing Friend Links

The `/friends` page is linked from the main navigation. Its introduction and
application instructions are editable in `src/content/page/friends.mdx`.
Visitors can suggest a link in the Giscus comments using this format:

```text
Site name:
Site URL:
Site introduction:
Avatar URL (optional):
```

Review submissions before adding them. Each published link is a YAML file in
`src/content/friend/`; use a unique filename and provide a name, URL, and short
description. The avatar is optional:

```yaml
name: "Example Blog"
url: "https://example.com"
description: "Notes on design, code, and the web."
avatar: "https://example.com/avatar.png"
darkAvatar: "https://example.com/avatar-dark.png"
```

`avatar` and `darkAvatar` are optional. Omit `avatar` to use the site's default
icon. If `darkAvatar` is provided, it is shown in dark mode while `avatar` (or
the default icon) is shown in light mode. Friend cards are generated from these
files and sorted by name. The comment section follows the global
`giscus.enabled` setting and the Giscus credentials in `site.config.ts`.

## 6. Keeping a Journal

Short notes and moments can be published on the Journal timeline without
appearing in the blog archive. Create a `.md` or `.mdx` file in
`src/content/journal/` with a title and date/time:

```markdown
---
title: "A small note"
pubDate: 2026-09-26
---

Write a short note here. Markdown content appears directly on the timeline.
```

Entries are sorted newest first. The date and time appear beside each entry on
the left; its title and content appear on the right. Use the same
`YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ss` format for blog posts and journal entries.

## 7. Commands

All commands are run from the root of the project, from a terminal:

| Command                | Action                                         |
| :--------------------- | :--------------------------------------------- |
| `pnpm install`         | Installs dependencies                          |
| `pnpm dev`             | Starts local dev server at `localhost:4321`    |
| `pnpm build`           | Builds your production site to `./dist/`       |
| `pnpm preview`         | Previews your build locally, before deploying  |
| `pnpm format`          | Formats code across the project using Prettier |
| `pnpm astro ...`       | Runs Astro CLI commands                        |
| `pnpm astro -- --help` | Gets help using the Astro CLI                  |

====================================================================================================
FILE: src/content/blog/markdown-style-guide.md
====================================================================================================
---
title: "Markdown Style Guide"
description: "Here is a sample of some basic Markdown syntax that can be used when writing Markdown content in Astro."
pubDate: 2026-08-28
featured: true
tags: [markdown, writing]
---

Here is a sample of some basic Markdown syntax that can be used when writing Markdown content in Astro.

## Headings

The following HTML `<h1>`—`<h6>` elements represent six levels of section headings. `<h1>` is the highest section level while `<h6>` is the lowest.

# H1

## H2

### H3

#### H4

##### H5

###### H6

## Images

### Syntax

```markdown
![Alt text](../../assets/preview.png)
```

### Output

![preview](../../assets/preview.png)

## Blockquotes

The blockquote element represents content that is quoted from another source, optionally with a citation which must be within a `footer` or `cite` element, and optionally with in-line changes such as annotations and abbreviations.

### Blockquote without attribution

#### Syntax

```markdown
> Tiam, ad mint andaepu dandae nostion secatur sequo quae.  
> **Note** that you can use _Markdown syntax_ within a blockquote.
```

#### Output

> Tiam, ad mint andaepu dandae nostion secatur sequo quae.  
> **Note** that you can use _Markdown syntax_ within a blockquote.

### Blockquote with attribution

#### Syntax

```markdown
> Don't communicate by sharing memory, share memory by communicating.<br />
> — <cite>Rob Pike[^1]</cite>
```

#### Output

> Don't communicate by sharing memory, share memory by communicating.<br />
> — <cite>Rob Pike[^1]</cite>

[^1]: The above quote is excerpted from Rob Pike's [talk](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PAAkCSZUG1c) during Gopherfest, November 18, 2015.

## GitHub-style alerts

Alerts are special blockquotes for highlighting notes, tips, and warnings. Use one of the five supported alert types.

### Syntax

```markdown
> [!NOTE]
> Useful information that readers should know.

> [!TIP]
> Helpful advice for doing something better.

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Key information readers need to know.

> [!WARNING]
> Urgent information that needs immediate attention.

> [!CAUTION]
> Advice about potential negative consequences.
```

### Output

> [!NOTE]
> Useful information that readers should know.

> [!TIP]
> Helpful advice for doing something better.

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Key information readers need to know.

> [!WARNING]
> Urgent information that needs immediate attention.

> [!CAUTION]
> Advice about potential negative consequences.

## Tables

### Syntax

```markdown
| Italics   | Bold     | Code   |
| --------- | -------- | ------ |
| _italics_ | **bold** | `code` |
```

### Output

| Italics   | Bold     | Code   |
| --------- | -------- | ------ |
| _italics_ | **bold** | `code` |

## Code Blocks

### Syntax

we can use 3 backticks ``` in new line and write snippet and close with 3 backticks on new line and to highlight language specific syntax, write one word of language name after first 3 backticks, for eg. html, javascript, css, markdown, typescript, txt, bash

````markdown
```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
  <head>
    <meta charset="utf-8" />
    <title>Example HTML5 Document</title>
  </head>
  <body>
    <p>Test</p>
  </body>
</html>
```
````

### Output

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
  <head>
    <meta charset="utf-8" />
    <title>Example HTML5 Document</title>
  </head>
  <body>
    <p>Test</p>
  </body>
</html>
```

## List Types

### Ordered List

#### Syntax

```markdown
1. First item
2. Second item
3. Third item
```

#### Output

1. First item
2. Second item
3. Third item

### Unordered List

#### Syntax

```markdown
- List item
- Another item
- And another item
```

#### Output

- List item
- Another item
- And another item

### Nested list

#### Syntax

```markdown
- Fruit
  - Apple
  - Orange
  - Banana
- Dairy
  - Milk
  - Cheese
```

#### Output

- Fruit
  - Apple
  - Orange
  - Banana
- Dairy
  - Milk
  - Cheese

## Other Elements — abbr, sub, sup, kbd, mark

### Syntax

```markdown
<abbr data-tooltip="Graphics Interchange Format">GIF</abbr> is a bitmap image format.

H<sub>2</sub>O

X<sup>n</sup> + Y<sup>n</sup> = Z<sup>n</sup>

Press <kbd>CTRL</kbd> + <kbd>ALT</kbd> + <kbd>Delete</kbd> to end the session.

Most <mark>salamanders</mark> are nocturnal, and hunt for insects, worms, and other small creatures.
```

### Output

<abbr data-tooltip="Graphics Interchange Format">GIF</abbr> is a bitmap image format.

H<sub>2</sub>O

X<sup>n</sup> + Y<sup>n</sup> = Z<sup>n</sup>

Press <kbd>CTRL</kbd> + <kbd>ALT</kbd> + <kbd>Delete</kbd> to end the session.

Most <mark>salamanders</mark> are nocturnal, and hunt for insects, worms, and other small creatures.

---
title: "About"
description: "A minimal, lightning-fast personal blog built with Astro."
---

## A calm place to publish

Mare Blog is a minimal, open-source blog built with Astro. It brings together
long-form **blog posts**, short **journal entries**, and standalone **pages**
in one lightweight site.

Pages are statically generated for fast loading. Find articles with built-in
full-text search, or switch between light and dark themes. Comments are powered by Giscus.

## Built with

- [Astro](https://astro.build)
- [Tailwind CSS](https://tailwindcss.com)
- [Pagefind](https://pagefind.app)
- [Giscus](https://giscus.app)

## Make it yours

You can deploy your own copy of Mare Blog to Cloudflare Pages and customize it.

[![Deploy to Cloudflare Pages](https://img.shields.io/badge/Deploy%20to-Cloudflare%20Pages-F38020?logo=cloudflare&logoColor=white)](https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/pages/new/provider/github)

Follow the [setup and configuration guide](/blog/guide/) to configure your
site, publish content, and connect Giscus.

The project is open source: [view the source on GitHub](https://github.com/Niceeepoiu/mare-blog).

## Apply for a link

Leave a comment below using this format:

```text
name: "Example Blog"
url: "https://example.com"
description: "Notes on design, code, and the web."
avatar: "https://example.com/avatar.png"
darkAvatar: "https://example.com/avatar-dark.png"
```

Please make sure your site is publicly accessible and the details are accurate. I review submissions periodically and may add or remove links as the list evolves.

FILE: src/content.config.ts
====================================================================================================
import { defineCollection } from "astro:content";
import { glob } from "astro/loaders";
import { z } from "astro/zod";

const blog = defineCollection({
  loader: glob({ pattern: "**/[^_]*.{md,mdx}", base: "./src/content/blog" }),
  schema: z.object({
    title: z.string(),
    description: z.string(),
    pubDate: z.coerce.date(),
    featured: z.boolean().default(false),
    tags: z.array(z.string().trim().min(1)).default([]),
  }),
});

const page = defineCollection({
  loader: glob({ pattern: "**/[^_]*.{md,mdx}", base: "./src/content/page" }),
  schema: z.object({
    title: z.string(),
    description: z.string().optional(),
  }),
});

const journal = defineCollection({
  loader: glob({ pattern: "**/[^_]*.{md,mdx}", base: "./src/content/journal" }),
  schema: z.object({
    title: z.string(),
    pubDate: z.coerce.date(),
  }),
});

const friend = defineCollection({
  loader: glob({ pattern: "**/*.yaml", base: "./src/content/friend" }),
  schema: z.object({
    name: z.string().trim().min(1),
    url: z.string().trim().min(1),
    description: z.string().trim().min(1),
    avatar: z.string().trim().min(1).optional(),
    darkAvatar: z.string().trim().min(1).optional(),
  }),
});

export const collections = { blog, page, journal, friend };

FILE: src/layouts/BaseLayout.astro
====================================================================================================
---
import siteConfig from "@config";
import "../styles/global.css";
import Header from "../components/Header.astro";
import Footer from "../components/Footer.astro";
import Comment from "../components/Comments.astro";
import GlobalTooltip from "../components/GlobalTooltip.astro";
import BackToTop from "../components/BackToTop.astro";

interface Props {
  pageTitle: string;
  description?: string;
  showComments?: boolean;
}

const { pageTitle, description, showComments = false } = Astro.props;
const metaDescription = description ?? siteConfig.description;
const lang = siteConfig.lang ?? "en";
---

<!doctype html>
<html lang={lang}>
  <head>
    <meta charset="utf-8" />
    <link rel="icon" type="image/svg+xml" href="/favicon.svg" />
    <link rel="icon" href="/favicon.ico" />
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
    <meta name="generator" content={Astro.generator} />
    <meta name="description" content={metaDescription} />
    <title>{pageTitle} - {siteConfig.title}</title>
    <script is:inline>
      (() => {
        let theme = null;
        try {
          theme = localStorage.getItem("theme");
        } catch {}
        const prefersDark = window.matchMedia(
          "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)",
        ).matches;
        const useDark = theme === "dark" || (theme !== "light" && prefersDark);
        document.documentElement.classList.toggle("dark", useDark);
      })();
    </script>
  </head>
  <body>
    <Header />
    <main class="mx-4 my-8 max-w-5xl sm:mx-6 lg:mx-auto" data-pagefind-body>
      <header
        class="mb-8 rounded-lg bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8 lg:p-10 dark:bg-gray-900"
      >
        {
          Astro.slots.has("page-header-prefix") && (
            <slot name="page-header-prefix" />
          )
        }
        <h1 class="my-0">{pageTitle}</h1>
        {
          description && (
            <p class="mt-3 text-base leading-relaxed text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">
              {description}
            </p>
          )
        }
        {
          Astro.slots.has("page-header-details") && (
            <div class={description ? "mt-4" : "mt-3"}>
              <slot name="page-header-details" />
            </div>
          )
        }
      </header>
      <slot />
    </main>
    {
      showComments && siteConfig.giscus.enabled && (
        <section class="mx-4 my-8 max-w-5xl sm:mx-6 lg:mx-auto">
          <Comment />
        </section>
      )
    }
    <BackToTop />
    <Footer />
    <GlobalTooltip />
  </body>
</html>

    props: { page },

import { getEntry, render } from "astro:content";
import siteConfig from "@config";
import AboutProfileCard from "../components/AboutProfileCard.astro";
import BaseLayout from "../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
import {
  fetchGitHubContributions,
  type GitHubContributionDay,
} from "../utils/github-contributions";

const aboutPage = await getEntry("page", "about");

if (!aboutPage) {
  throw new Error("The About page content entry is missing.");
}

const { Content } = await render(aboutPage);

let contributions: GitHubContributionDay[] = [];
let contributionError: string | undefined;

try {
  contributions = await fetchGitHubContributions(siteConfig.githubUsername);
} catch (error) {
  console.error(
    `Unable to load GitHub contributions for @${siteConfig.githubUsername}:`,
    error,
  );
  contributionError =
    "GitHub contribution data is currently unavailable. Please try again later.";
}
---

<BaseLayout
  pageTitle={aboutPage.data.title}
  description={aboutPage.data.description}
>
  <AboutProfileCard
    contributions={contributions}
    contributionError={contributionError}
  />
  <article
    class="markdown-content rounded-lg bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-8 lg:p-12 dark:bg-gray-900"
  >
    <Content />
  </article>
</BaseLayout>

import { Icon } from "astro-icon/components";
import type { CollectionEntry } from "astro:content";
import type { GetStaticPaths, Page } from "astro";
import siteConfig from "@config";
import BaseLayout from "../../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
import PostCard from "../../components/PostCard.astro";
import { getBlogPosts } from "../../utils/content";

export const getStaticPaths = (async ({ paginate }) => {
  const posts = await getBlogPosts();

  return paginate(posts, {
    pageSize: siteConfig.postsPerPage ?? 6,
  });
}) satisfies GetStaticPaths;

type Props = {
  page: Page<CollectionEntry<"blog">>;
};

const { page } = Astro.props;
const pageTitle =
  page.currentPage === 1 ? "Blog" : `Blog - Page ${page.currentPage}`;
const pageLinks = Array.from(
  { length: page.lastPage },
  (_, index) => index + 1,
);

      <div
        class="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 transition-colors duration-200 group-hover:bg-blue-500 group-hover:text-white dark:bg-gray-800 dark:text-gray-400"

          data-tooltip="No next page"

        >
          <Icon name="lucide:chevron-right" class="h-4 w-4" />
        </span>
      )
    }
  </nav>
</BaseLayout>

    params: { slug: post.id },

  pageTitle={post.data.title}

  showComments={true}

      datetime={formatISODate(post.data.pubDate)}

                <Icon
                  name="lucide:tag"
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  class="mr-1 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0"

                <span>{tag}</span>
              </a>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )
    }
  </div>
  <article
    class="markdown-content rounded-lg bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-8 lg:p-12 dark:bg-gray-900"
  >
    <Content />
  </article>
</BaseLayout>

import BaseLayout from "../../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
import {
  formatISODate,
  formatMonthDay,
  getBlogPosts,
} from "../../utils/content";

const posts = await getBlogPosts();

const groupedPosts = Object.groupBy(posts, (post) =>
  String(post.data.pubDate.getUTCFullYear()),
);

const archiveEntries = (
  Object.entries(groupedPosts) as [string, typeof posts][]
).sort(([yearA], [yearB]) => Number(yearB) - Number(yearA));
---

import type { CollectionEntry } from "astro:content";
import type { GetStaticPaths, Page } from "astro";
import { Icon } from "astro-icon/components";
import siteConfig from "@config";
import BaseLayout from "../../../../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
import PostCard from "../../../../components/PostCard.astro";
import {
  getPostsForTag,
  getTagHref,
  getTagSummaries,
} from "../../../../utils/tags";
import { getBlogPosts } from "../../../../utils/content";

export const getStaticPaths = (async ({ paginate }) => {
  const allPosts = await getBlogPosts();
  const tags = getTagSummaries(allPosts);
  const pageSize = Math.max(1, Math.floor(siteConfig.postsPerPage ?? 6));

  return tags.flatMap((tag) => {
    const posts = getPostsForTag(allPosts, tag.slug);

    return paginate(posts, {
      params: { tag: tag.slug },
      pageSize,
      props: { tagName: tag.name, totalPosts: posts.length },
    });
  });
}) satisfies GetStaticPaths;

type Props = {
  page: Page<CollectionEntry<"blog">>;
  tagName: string;
  totalPosts: number;
};

const { page, tagName, totalPosts } = Astro.props;
const pageLinks = Array.from(
  { length: page.lastPage },
  (_, index) => index + 1,
);
const tagHref = getTagHref(tagName);
const description =
  page.currentPage === 1
    ? `${totalPosts} ${totalPosts === 1 ? "article" : "articles"} with this tag`
    : undefined;
---

<BaseLayout
  pageTitle={`Tag: ${tagName}${page.currentPage > 1 ? ` - Page ${page.currentPage}` : ""}`}
  description={description}
>
  <div slot="page-header-prefix">
    <p class="text-md font-medium text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">
      <a href="/blog/tags" class="hover:text-blue-600 dark:hover:text-blue-400">
        Back to tags
      </a>
    </p>
  </div>
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-6">
    {page.data.map((post) => <PostCard post={post} />)}
  </div>
  <nav
    aria-label="Tag archive pagination"
    class="mt-8 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm"
  >
    {
      page.url.prev ? (
        <a
          href={page.url.prev}
          data-tooltip="Previous page"
          aria-label="Previous page"
          class="inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-gray-700 transition-colors duration-200 hover:border-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50 hover:text-blue-600 dark:border-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200 dark:hover:border-blue-500 dark:hover:bg-gray-800 dark:hover:text-blue-400"
        >
          <Icon name="lucide:chevron-left" class="h-4 w-4" />
        </a>
      ) : (
        <span
          data-tooltip="No previous page"
          aria-label="Previous page"
          class="inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-gray-100 text-gray-400 dark:border-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-800 dark:text-gray-600"
        >
          <Icon name="lucide:chevron-left" class="h-4 w-4" />
        </span>
      )
    }
    <div
      class="inline-flex rounded-lg border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700"
    >
      {
        pageLinks.map((pageNumber) => {
          const href = pageNumber === 1 ? tagHref : `${tagHref}/${pageNumber}`;
          const isCurrent = page.currentPage === pageNumber;
          return (
            <a
              href={href}
              aria-current={isCurrent ? "page" : undefined}
              class={`relative flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center border-r border-gray-200 text-sm transition-colors duration-200 first:rounded-l-lg last:rounded-r-lg last:border-r-0 dark:border-gray-700 ${
                isCurrent
                  ? "z-10 bg-blue-600 font-medium text-white"
                  : "bg-white text-gray-700 hover:bg-blue-50 hover:text-blue-600 dark:bg-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-800 dark:hover:text-blue-400"
              }`}
            >
              {pageNumber}
            </a>
          );
        })
      }
    </div>
    {
      page.url.next ? (
        <a
          href={page.url.next}
          data-tooltip="Next page"
          aria-label="Next page"
          class="inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white text-gray-700 transition-colors duration-200 hover:border-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50 hover:text-blue-600 dark:border-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200 dark:hover:border-blue-500 dark:hover:bg-gray-800 dark:hover:text-blue-400"
        >
          <Icon name="lucide:chevron-right" class="h-4 w-4" />
        </a>
      ) : (
        <span
          data-tooltip="No next page"
          aria-label="Next page"
          class="inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-gray-100 text-gray-400 dark:border-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-800 dark:text-gray-600"
        >
          <Icon name="lucide:chevron-right" class="h-4 w-4" />
        </span>
      )
    }
  </nav>
</BaseLayout>

<script>
  const shuffleFriendCards = () => {
    const grid = document.querySelector("[data-friend-grid]");
    if (!(grid instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

    const cards = Array.from(grid.children);
    for (let index = cards.length - 1; index > 0; index--) {
      const randomIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * (index + 1));
      [cards[index], cards[randomIndex]] = [cards[randomIndex], cards[index]];
    }

    grid.replaceChildren(...cards);
  };

  shuffleFriendCards();
  document.addEventListener("astro:page-load", shuffleFriendCards);
</script>

import { render } from "astro:content";
import BaseLayout from "../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
import {
  formatISODate,
  formatMonthDay,
  getJournalEntries,
} from "../utils/content";

const entries = await getJournalEntries();

const renderedEntries = await Promise.all(
  entries.map(async (entry) => {
    const { Content } = await render(entry);

    return {
      entry,
      Content,
    };
  }),
);

const entriesByYear = new Map<number, typeof renderedEntries>();

for (const item of renderedEntries) {
  const year = item.entry.data.pubDate.getUTCFullYear();
  const yearEntries = entriesByYear.get(year) ?? [];

  yearEntries.push(item);
  entriesByYear.set(year, yearEntries);
}

const journalYears = [...entriesByYear.entries()];
---

<BaseLayout
  pageTitle="Journal"
  description="Short notes and moments collected over time."
>
  {
    renderedEntries.length > 0 ? (

        <div class="flex flex-col gap-7 sm:gap-8">
          {journalYears.map(([year, yearEntries]) => (
            <section
              aria-labelledby={`journal-year-${year}`}
              class="relative before:absolute before:top-5 before:bottom-0 before:left-20 before:border-l before:border-gray-300 before:content-[''] sm:before:top-5.75 dark:before:border-gray-700"
            >
              <h2
                id={`journal-year-${year}`}
                class="relative grid grid-cols-[3.5rem_3rem_minmax(0,1fr)] items-center text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-gray-800 sm:text-2xl dark:text-gray-100"
              >
                <span>{year}</span>
                <span class="flex justify-center">
                  <span class="relative z-10 h-3.5 w-3.5 rounded-full border-2 border-blue-500 bg-white dark:border-blue-400 dark:bg-gray-900" />
                </span>
                <span class="text-xs font-normal tracking-normal text-gray-500 sm:text-sm dark:text-gray-400">

                  {yearEntries.length === 1 ? "entry" : "entries"}

FILE: src/pages/robots.txt.ts
====================================================================================================
import type { APIRoute } from "astro";

export const GET: APIRoute = ({ site }) => {
  const sitemapURL = site ? new URL("sitemap-index.xml", site).href : null;

  const content = [
    "User-agent: *",
    "Allow: /",
    ...(sitemapURL ? ["", `Sitemap: ${sitemapURL}`] : []),
  ].join("\n");

  return new Response(content, {
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
      "Cache-Control": "public, max-age=3600, must-revalidate",
    },
  });
};

FILE: src/pages/rss.xml.ts
====================================================================================================
import rss from "@astrojs/rss";
import type { APIContext } from "astro";
import siteConfig from "@config";
import { getBlogPosts } from "../utils/content";

export async function GET(context: APIContext) {
  const posts = await getBlogPosts();

  return rss({
    title: siteConfig.title,
    description: siteConfig.description || "Niceeepoiu's Personal Blog",
    site: context.site!,
    items: posts.map((post) => ({
      title: post.data.title,
      pubDate: post.data.pubDate,
      description: post.data.description,
      link: `/blog/${post.id}/`,
    })),
    customData: `<language>en</language>`,
  });
}
